{"step_index":11015,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-05-17T07:01:36Z","content":"You're right to question that. Here's when that happens:\n\n**`needsInternetVerification = true` AND `hasAccess = true`** occurs when the user has an **active offline claim**. They get full app access for 24 hours without any internet check.\n\nThe concern: during those 24 hours, the app **never verifies with the server**. So if the admin revoked their premium during that window, the user wouldn't know until the claim expires.\n\nWorse — a user could theoretically claim all 10 offline days **without ever connecting to the server to verify their subscription status**.\n\nShould I tighten this so that:\n- When the app **does** have internet during an active claim, it still verifies with the server (records online check + checks for revocations)?\n- The offline claim only **bypasses the block screen**, not the verification attempt?\n\nThat way the claim just prevents the user from being locked out, but the app still tries to phone home whenever it can."}
